**Its families, the family with the heaviest hitter first.**

- **Facility & Grid Optimization** (15 here): plant `energy_storage`, a battery, UPS or microgrid store with a load and a grid connection; class `facility`, a facility battery behind the meter, reached through the energy management system. Native: the facility's energy management system and demand-response gateway (OpenADR). Run live? modelled.
- **Energy Storage & Microgrid** (19 here): plant `energy_storage`, a battery, UPS or microgrid store with a load and a grid connection; class `microgrid`, a microgrid battery and loads, reached through the inverter or energy management system (IEEE 2030.5, SunSpec, OpenADR). Native: the battery's or site's energy management system and inverter (SunSpec, IEEE 2030.5, OpenADR, Modbus). Run live? modelled: CityLearn (results/live/V3_CITYLEARN.md) for the districts; the register path is written.
- **PDU, UPS & Electrical Distribution** (18 here): plant `energy_storage`, a battery, UPS or microgrid store with a load and a grid connection; class `ups`, an uninterruptible power supply, reached through the UPS and PDU management interface (SNMP, Modbus). Native: the UPS and PDU management interfaces (SNMP, Modbus) and the servers' BMC power caps (IPMI DCMI, Redfish). Run live? modelled.
- **Industrial PLC & Process Automation** (18 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `process`, an industrial process loop, reached through the PLC or DCS (OPC UA, EtherNet/IP, PROFINET). Native: the PLC or DCS loop (OPC UA, EtherNet/IP, PROFINET): the setpoint and the mode of each loop. Run live? modelled.
- **Cooling, Chillers & Thermodynamics** (18 here): plant `thermal_zone`, a data hall or building zone cooled by chillers, air handlers or fans; class `data_hall`, a data hall cooling plant, reached through the building management system (BACnet, Modbus) or its command line. Native: the building management system and the chiller plant's own supervisory controller (BACnet, Modbus). Run live? modelled (the data-hall plant); the BACnet path is written.
- **Building & Critical Environment HVAC** (15 here): plant `thermal_zone`, a data hall or building zone cooled by chillers, air handlers or fans; class `building`, a building zone, reached through the building management system. Native: the building management system (BACnet) and the AHU and zone controllers. Run live? modelled: CityLearn districts (results/live/V3_CITYLEARN.md); the BACnet path is written.
- **Healthcare Critical Environments** (13 here): plant `thermal_zone`, a data hall or building zone cooled by chillers, air handlers or fans; class `hospital`, a hospital's critical rooms (operating rooms, isolation, pharmacy, imaging suites), reached through the hospital's building management system (BACnet). Native: the hospital's building management system (BACnet) with its clinical limits. Run live? modelled only.
- **Agriculture & Irrigation** (14 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `irrigation`, pumps, pressure and climate on a farm or in a greenhouse, reached through the pump station controller, the pivot panel or the climate computer (Modbus, the vendor cloud). Native: the pump station controller, pivot panel or climate computer (Modbus, the vendor cloud). Run live? modelled only.
- **Oil & Gas Pipelines** (14 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `pipeline`, a pipeline segment with its compressors, pumps and valves, reached through pipeline SCADA (DNP3, Modbus, OPC UA). Native: pipeline SCADA (DNP3, Modbus, OPC UA). Run live? modelled only.
- **Mining & Mineral Processing** (14 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `mill`, a grinding, flotation or materials-handling circuit, reached through the plant DCS or PLC (OPC UA). Native: the plant DCS or PLC (OPC UA). Run live? modelled only.
- **District Heating & Cooling** (13 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `district_heat`, a district heating or cooling network, reached through the network control system and substation controllers (Modbus, M-Bus). Native: the network control system and substation controllers (Modbus, M-Bus). Run live? modelled only.
- **Power Generation & Turbine Control** (14 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `turbine`, a generating unit's governor, boiler or excitation loop, reached through the plant DCS and governor (OPC UA, IEC 61850; modelled only). Native: the plant DCS and the unit's governor (OPC UA, IEC 61850). Run live? modelled only.
- **Renewable Generation & Inverter Control** (13 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `inverter`, a plant of grid-support inverters or wind turbines, reached through the plant controller and inverter settings (SunSpec, IEC 61850, IEEE 1547). Native: the plant controller and inverter settings (SunSpec, IEC 61850, IEEE 1547). Run live? modelled: SimBench grids solved by pandapower (results/live/V3_PANDAPOWER.md); the register path is written.
- **Pharmaceutical & Food Manufacturing** (14 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `batch_reactor`, a GMP batch reactor, fermenter or food process, reached through the batch control system (ISA-88, OPC UA). Native: the batch control system (ISA-88, OPC UA) with the validated recipe limits. Run live? modelled only.
- **Grid Transmission & Distribution** (12 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `feeder_voltage`, a distribution feeder's voltage, reached through the distribution management system (IEC 61850, DNP3; modelled only). Native: the distribution management system and the devices' own controllers (IEC 61850, DNP3): regulators, capacitor banks, inverters. Run live? modelled: SimBench grids (results/live/V3_PANDAPOWER.md).
- **Semiconductor Fab & Precision Manufacturing** (13 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `chamber`, a controlled chamber (clean room, kiln, reactor), reached through the PLC or DCS. Native: the fab's MES and each tool's controller (SECS/GEM). Run live? modelled only.
- **Water Wastewater & Pumping** (12 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `water`, a water or pumping process, reached through the SCADA system (Modbus, DNP3). Native: the utility's SCADA (Modbus, DNP3) and the pump station controllers. Run live? modelled only.

**Its families, the family with the heaviest hitter first.**

- **Database & Transactions** (19 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `database`, a database cluster, reached through the database operator (replicas, connection pools). Native: the database's own pooler and server settings: PgBouncer's admin console, MySQL SET GLOBAL, MongoDB setParameter, the database operator. Run live? yes, tools/run_pgbench.py, run_sysbench.py, run_ycsb.py, three-run tables V3_PGBENCH, V3_SYSBENCH, V3_YCSB.
- **Commerce & Payment Systems** (15 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `commerce`, a customer-facing service with bursts, reached through the Kubernetes API. Native: the payment platform's gateway and its routing rules. Run live? modelled.
- **Medical Imaging & Clinical Systems** (12 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `clinical`, clinical systems: imaging archives, records, interface engines, monitoring gateways, reached through the application's own scaling and admission settings (Kubernetes, the vendor console). Native: the clinical application's own scaling and admission settings (Kubernetes, the vendor console). Run live? modelled only.
- **Financial Markets & Trading Systems** (14 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the trading platform's gateway, matching engine and risk settings, the colocation BMS. Run live? modelled only.
- **Messaging & Streaming** (22 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the broker's own controls: Kafka's admin API and consumer group, RabbitMQ policies, MQTT broker settings. Run live? yes, tools/run_kafka.py, V3_KAFKA.
- **Service Mesh & API Reliability** (16 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the mesh's own policies: Istio DestinationRule and VirtualService, Linkerd ServiceProfile, Envoy filters. Run live? modelled; the API path is kubectl patch.
- **Runtime & Application** (21 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the application runtime's own settings: thread pools, JVM heap and GC, worker counts, connection pools. Run live? modelled.
- **Observability & Telemetry** (9 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the collector's own configuration: the OpenTelemetry Collector, Prometheus scrape and remote-write settings. Run live? modelled.
- **Reliability, Security & Recovery** (18 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the platform's own recovery and security controls: rollout and rollback, PodDisruptionBudgets, the security hold, a kill switch. Run live? in part, the security hold and kill switch run in every Kubernetes benchmark (omnicompass/master.py).
- **Cache & Memory Services** (16 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the cache's own configuration: Redis CONFIG SET, Memcached runtime settings. Run live? yes, tools/run_redis.py, V3_REDIS.
- **Search, Indexing & Vector DB** (16 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `server`, an application service on servers or pods, reached through the Kubernetes API (HPA target and floor, pod resize). Native: the search engine's settings API: OpenSearch/Elasticsearch cluster and index settings, the vector database's config. Run live? modelled.
- **Telecom RAN & Edge Radio** (12 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `ran`, a radio access network cell or site, reached through the RAN controller (O-RAN interfaces). Native: the RAN's near-real-time RIC and O-RAN interfaces (E2, O1). Run live? modelled only.
- **Storage Block/File/Object** (20 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `storage`, block, file or object storage, reached through the storage system's QoS and tiering API. Native: the storage system's QoS and tiering: Ceph (ceph osd pool set, QoS), a cloud volume's IOPS class, S3 lifecycle. Run live? modelled.
- **Network Routing & Switching** (19 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `network`, network routing and switching, reached through the network controller (SDN, routing API). Native: the network controller: the load balancer's weights, the SDN controller, the NIC's ethtool and tc settings. Run live? modelled.
- **Content Delivery & Edge Cache** (6 here): plant `process_loop`, a regulated process: a temperature, pressure, flow, level, voltage or composition held at a setpoint; class `process`, an industrial process loop, reached through the PLC or DCS (OPC UA, EtherNet/IP, PROFINET). Native: the CDN's configuration API and the edge cache's settings. Run live? modelled.
- **Workflow, Logistics & Fulfillment** (15 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `workflow`, a workflow or pipeline engine, reached through the workflow engine's concurrency settings. Native: the workflow engine (Temporal, Airflow, Camunda) and the fulfilment system's dispatch rules. Run live? modelled.
- **Data Analytics & ETL** (15 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `batch`, batch and queued jobs, reached through the job queue (Kubernetes Jobs, Slurm, Spark). Native: Spark's dynamic allocation and the SQL engine's slots (spark.dynamicAllocation, Flink's parallelism, warehouse slots). Run live? modelled.
- **Ports & Maritime Logistics** (12 here): plant `compute_pool`, a pool of servers, pods, GPUs, links or disks serving a stream of requests or jobs; class `port`, a container terminal's cranes and vehicles taking moves, reached through the terminal operating system and fleet controllers. Native: the terminal operating system and the equipment fleet controllers. Run live? modelled only.
